TALATCHENGANl 
Pagitchamrangni bang a aganritinganirang gnangchim. Uamang A chik a doko nape 
songdong a chachengaoni maidakgiparangko chagrongachlnt unikoa antangtangni mikgronchi 
mairangko nikahachim uarangko deritchuna aganritinge donanga bang-achino Ua 
aganntinggipa biap indakeming ku bisringni kri gittimsaoni gittimsaona on-tiprak dingtanggrikani 
donga 
Pagitchamrang Mitdeni ma kringoni Mandeni ma-kringona uamangni dingtang 
dingtang biap unia chasongo chagronggimin a selrango pangchake aganronga. Indiba ua 
aganntinganiko am rikkite seachi ripinga dongjani gimin bri ja delgipa pagitcham

ma gitchamrang baksan gimachapaengaha. Chengoni kattarangko rongtale ma-rape aganna 
man gipa bilsi bngipa manderangko man naba neng aha Indaken ka sinsin pagitchamrangni 
gamchatgipa gisikni gam uamang baksan sichapangengaha. 
la bijangchi chasongo adita skia man giparang ong katgenchimoba jangchio 
manderang Knstianni gadango bate chanchie pagitchamrangni kattana gisik nangbrejaha. 
Bang batan pagitchamni kattarangko mande gitchamrangni chanchiani aro bebe ra-ani inesa 
mikchepe ra tokaha Indake an tangtangni dakbewal, rikbewal, bebe ra ani unia ku-sik gimikan 
apsan apsan pagitchamrang baksa re chapangengaha. 
Oa ororo pagitchamni kattarangko sandirikna ja ku ga-taengaha. An tangtangni 
dakbewal. ja rikani unia ku sikko namdapatan baksa chimonge ripingnaba gisik nangbaengaha. 
Indiba mitamrangko man na neng beam ong aha. Maina, chasongni kri, nika niani apaldapani 
kn. an tangtangni ku sik aro aganritinganiko dingtangataha. Uni gimin, da-ororo chengoni 
pagitchamni gamchatgipa gisikni jakra gamko man-na neng aha. 
Anga noksik nokbakoniko badita pagitchamrangni kattako manderangoniko knarika 
uarangko chimonge tarina chanchiaha. Ua chimonggimin kattarangko ‘Katta Wdl tim* ine 
donaha Ua kattarangko mitamko angna agantimgipani aganako ja-rike seaha mitamko 
an tangni ku sikchi tarie seaha. Pagitchamni want! jakra gita ong-pajaoba, ma-apani rama 
pnika gita. songsul noksuini bewalko nikrikpaa gita, chimonggimin kattarangko bimang 
ong atna tikkelachi chu sokatna man aha 
larang pilakan pagitchamrangni cha spako e chamdamoniko am skila gita, ringa 
bon chakgijako sreko dikchok de galaoniko chubisre we skile ringrika gita, iano angni ku sik 
brinbawe jakkalbrine sedomikgipa kattarangko indita me-sue nikjawaha. Indiba ian pagitchamni 
gamchatgipa gisikni gamrangni bisnokko chimongrika gita ong-gen ine anga ka-donga. 
la noksik nokbakni kattarangko chimongna angna bang-a manderang dakchakaha. 
Uamangoni Dhiresing Ch. Marak, Chong rin G. Marak unia Lodrik R. Sangma ong-a. Angni 
ripeng Sailang R. Marak mongsonggipa on gilgipa ong aha. Unbaksana angni ka sagipa 
jikgipa Belbina R. Sangmani dakchakachiba bang-a kattarangko iano chaprimatna man aha. 
la angni kan-dik kan diktarigimin kattarang banoba saniba knani gita apsan ong janaba 
gnang unia angni jakkalgipa ku-sikko me-sunikjanaba donga. Indiba iarang piiakan pagitchamni 
gamchatgipa gitcham gisikni gamko ripingani ine ra-pachina poraigipa sakantikon 
mol molchenga, Mittela.

1. KATCHI KNI TOM GUA 
Pagitcham ma gitchamni ku bisnngoni indake ong kata Chengo 
patgitcham ma gitchamrangni Songdu chibimako ga batna apnikjae akaweioliol 
re jojo am jojo dakmitingo, bobtini mil lam ku pachi duulaoniko ginalni jakpra 
ritchok chakani bijangchioniko, chri miksik gnangko kebite re a ja ku dilgipa, 
bra janggi gnangko ra bite rama ja kol pnikgipa gnangchim. Ua rama dilgipa 
ua ja kol pnikgipakoara ku rangkoa knana man a indiba bimangko nikna 
manjachtm Ua chri miksik gnangko kebitgipa bra janggi gnangko 
ra bitgipakoara Katchi Gando Gangija Jugu Km Bikgija mesa mingtokaiachim. 
Pagitchamrang uko Nirikgipa Rodilgipa unia ja-ku dilgipa ‘Saljong Panta 
Saljong Mitde” minge okamachim Gisep gipu bobil dingo dingnangja ginal 
keo kenangjani salo, pagitchamrangko dilgipa ua Saljong Panteko okam 
ringsale maiko dako namgnok maiko ka o ke gnok uo sing rongachim. Unosa 
ua Jugu Katchiba dakna ka na nanggniko ku patti ku rarone on rongachim. 
Ua Saljong Mitde - Katchi Gando Gangija Jugu Kni Tom gija - chri 
miksik gnangko kee bra janggi gnangko ra e pagitchamna bobil dingchakode 
ginal kechakode, bilakgipa bobilba wa sagipa ginalba amchak sokchakjachim. 
Indake pagitchamrangko re a ja kuprako nigual nisangwalgija skang 
re e mikkang dile, bobil dingan salo dingchake, ginal kean salo kechake. bri 
mikka simdim a ding mikkim tangsim niksokja nisokja dakgipa a brichipakna 
mikkange ua Songdu a kaweoniko re dil dodilbajok. 
Pagitchamrangni uno ga kat gadobachengoara, bolma bolgrim ritchok 
su a gita chonggi batchanga gita, bolma dru onana wa ma kongsi nangana, 
bol bingtipa budu wegripa bring napna buchu nikjajok; a bri dona chol 
man jajok Una bolma bing tip budu wetip dakgipako bring napna ramana 
den kre o kree nioba den ani adi ja man bol ritdimdopilaijok, budu den-sot 
galani tapan budu ritchupilaijok 
Uchi bolma andalpile dikgipao, ua chiring ka sinjrimjrim jokenggipao,

siksak siksak wa bijak siksak siksak ja biranga gam-a ku-aganakoa knira 
bimangkoa nikja dakgiparangsa songdong a-chasoengachim Bring adita 
diltukaiachi napsikangoan Abet rim-sojok Chiring bolma andaij^pacttf 
soksrapgijan Chisik doksojok.

Pagitchamrang ua mitde banggipa bolma bolgrimko chisik gapgipa chiringko 
gale re giminchi re-pilna ja koltangko an pilna ine chanchitokjokchim. 
Indiba ua Saljong Pante, pagitcham ma gitchamko do-ga chakdilgipa 
krok saidilgipana, “Da-ode na-song bachi an-pilkuna bachi katpilkuna? 
Na-songna donsogipa a-dokona sokjok, Na-songna chamsogipa chigaona 
najok! Injon songdong a chana nangainok. Ikon chiga chiringna nangainok!” 
ine agan pattiaha. 
Unoba pagitqhamrang, “Aiao! Indake pring mitde rim-aiode, an-tam 
chisik dokaiode, bano janggi tangna bano jama rakna? Indakjringaiode 
chingade ma-kimagne ma-chong bon-gne!” ine songdongna jechakaiachim. 
Indake pagitchamrangni kena jajrengako nike, “Na-songa ua Abet 
Rangge ua Chisik Mitde ming ripeng dakaibo besa mong-aiboda! Ang-ke 
mangsa man-ako chelwatgrike*’, na chi mangsa man ako ratonggrike sual 
antie ku-mong nangrimgrikaiboda! Indake noksul rogrikaibo a sul cha grikaibo!" 
ine ku- pattiaha.

noksul rorimna a-sul cha-rimna ine ang-ke mangsa man-ako chelprak 
pakchitgrike na-chi mangsa rim-ako tong-prak sualghke ku mongna nangrimna 
a-bachengjok.

dokoa, mitdena sima nijok, chisikna kodi*® galjok. Kamal sima nio mitdena 
chaoa kodi galo chisikna inoa sambasie - bengraru songe, re su sokgitdolko'*^ 
e-chu ane, damani pal wa-singko dama ping-e, rangni pal boltottu bijakko 
kare sike, mil-lamni pal gol pilakko sambasigipa wa toko pote - wa pepe sike 
okam ringsale krit an-sikjok. Indakesa Salna do-bok sale mesoke, Misina 
Saljongna de e pnike, sagipana kimil pa-e an chiko bengraruna toke kritaha. 
Uni ja-mansa pagitchamrang nokkap o na skang samdang den-na skang

jajumang jakariclii beng-a kanga griosa uko nokkap o e nokrik nql^akjok.

pagitchamrangni da o songdong a chaenggipa a bri adokona napbachanggipa 
rama choiko da onaba “Saljong Batram Rong dogachol” ba “Satjangbatra 
(Sal jang-batra) Rong dogachol" mingenga. 
Unosa A chik pagitchamrang bachi anpilkuna bachi katpilkuna ineming 
regringko den chite bijasikko so chipe, dru songe wa ma ge e, a song biming 
minge kosi rong ma songe, salna do bok okgnang den e a na cha ko 
panggnang songe; iano songdongainok inon a chainok, chiga chi ringainok 
ine a song kritjok, kosi tatchengjok. Indake a song krite kosi wantok toke 
a song songdongo chiga chi ringoa, rong dotma dotsako wiiwile jamang** 
o oan a tot dakoan mia misi silchajok, chal ik baring nangjok. Rong'dotma 
dotsako wilwile jamang gamgipaonikon mi misi jam gape man tokjok. 
Uko nikesa uchi Katchi gando gangija Jugu kni tom gija Saljong Mitde 
aganjok. “Chingaba basakona rodamkuna basakona cha damkuna! Da-ode 
re pil dopilna nangaignok Na song inon songdong a-chae game rim-e 
cha aibojok Inon mia misi man-amok. Na songba jama janggi rakainok.*

ong taijok, “Aiao...da oara* Salsa salisrapgija walsa waltumangija basak 
da ogalgal pil gnok?”

watatanasa re dilbapaia! Na songba na songni sokna nanggipaona solgok, 
nana nanggipaona najok. Da ode inon songdongaibojok. Inon a chaibojokl” 
Unosa pagitchamrang an tangtangko watgal dongale re-angna am-ana 
jajrenge, "Aia! Intang jawa bobil dingna rikbaode, ja-man gipin ginat sane 
nabaode, chingnara sawa dingchakpanok? Sawa dakchakpanok?” 
Una Saljong Pante, “Saoba bobil dingbaode, saoba ginal sanbaode, 
a ko ga timtime salko jaksi ote 'O Saljong! Mikgron otboail Ja-dok pe-boail'

inataibo. Chinga rikbai mikgron otaigne, ja dok pe-aigne!” ine ku-pattiaha.

pagitchamrang an tangtangni den bilsie o ritchoke gam e ge e man-chenggipa 
mi misiko an tangtang ku o tapchenggija, ua Misina ua Saljongna, maljurio 
e chu ane cha similo cha chat so e ginde galchenga, e chuo chingaichengjok. 
Ja manosa an tanatanode uko ku o siia cha chikaha. 
Pagitchamngni aganani ja kuko nirikode, Misi Saljong unia Ba bra inode 
uan gimikni kosako dal batgipa ong a. lano janapgipa Saipng Pante ba Saipng 
Mitde, pko pagitchamrang Jugu Kni Tom gip Katchi Gando Gangip ine minga, 
uade watatako man e pagitchamrangko rama dilgipasan ong aia. Uni giminsa 
uko Saipng Pante ba Saipng Mitde ine okamaha. Uno Saipng Panteni 
pagitchamrangna ku pattianga gitaba an tangna dahbat chu batgipanasa 
chingal rugalchina aganangaha. Uandake Misina Saljongna chingal 
rugalatgipan bisongnaba ong paignok ineba agandapangaha. 
2. SALGRA GITDOK CHIKA 
Pagitchamrang jeko Mitdeni ma kring ine chana uarangko dingtang 
dingtang biming dona. Misi Saljong inode uan dal-batgipa ong a. Ba bra 
Kalkame mandeko dake rue una baljanggiko sikate watatgipa ong- a. 
Mitamrang mandeko dake rue janggi silchi sikatgipa Ba bra Kalkambe 
indakeming uko ba mumu ba-toptope nirikgipako Jikmitde Kalkame ineba 
minga. 
Mande unia a-ani kosako rogipa mitderangni kosako bil man-gipa uan 
Salgra ming Susime ong-a. Pilakchi ja rurana man-gipa unia a-ni prem chini

jim-jemni kosako bil man-gipa unikoa mande ming mitdena mikkang pa e 
aganna pattina man-gipa Susime jeko Chisako Katchi Susime ina unia 
Matchidualo Jarura Mitde Susime unia Ruragipa Susime ineba minga. Salgra 
unia Susimeni mamatangrang Biskromming uni adatang Singsing Nokma 
Gansing Racha ong-a. Biskroman mandena gam unia rasongko pattina 
man-gipa ong-a. Game rim e cha-gipa a-galmak krito Misi Saljongko okam 
ringsalo a-ni Biskrom Bidotaruko mingchape okama. Uno Biskrom knachakosa 
bini ka dingatosa a-bao mia misi silchae nama mandeba man-e pake rasong 
man-a. Ukosa me-aparang a-ba bolchu chadinga Biskrom ka-dinga ina. 
Chengo Biskromni nok sa-rao Aning Rai Raji Gisiksan Pante nokpante 
rikachim. Ua nokpanteko rika matchoto nokdong gana Biskromni sa grerang 
Miska, Pabina, Menasi, Ragina. Noreru, Kileru, Ajering, Dingjenng. Sandini, 
Mandini gimikan nokpanteo gapjolachim. Ua Biskromni sa-grerangna Saigra 
dal-batgipa ong-achim.

bang pretachim. Unochacha A anmani mamatang. Biskromni adatang. 
Singsing Nokma Gansing Racha rikgitdok ganjoree siliting gobikbake, a na 
dolma stike saina satti winchike re baengachim. 
Uko niksoe, sawa chu ringchenggne ine, chu tokkolo da* bite bakpong 
jako ra bite kanoba, nokpante gapjoigipa Biskromni sa-grerang darangba 
ringna pa jaha Unoa Salgra, "Ra-ba angchi! Angan Saigra grikao ba-gipa 
jasengao neng gipa! A-song baliim a change angkoan ba a, chiga a-an chiboisK) 
angkoan neng-a! Menggo ma ko jakskil raram Salgrana rangkara onram a-song 
baliim a chang chiga aru gittimo angkon ba chenga! Anga Salgra 
ring chenggne!" ine Saigra mamatang o-gipako dondape ringchengaha 
Uno mamatang o gipa dalbatgipako seng-napgija, an-tangko grikao 
ba gipa jasengao neng gipa ine, Singsing Nokma Gansing Rachako dOTKlapa 
chu ringoa, rang san, Salgrani gingkoloni snare manggimik ong-ka^olaijok. 
Unosa Saigra, mamatang ogipako*^ dondape chu ringani indake marang 
gajok ine ma-siaha. Unasa mamatangko dondape o-gipako chonnike dakade 
marang ong a ine Singsing Nokma Gansing Racha nokpanteona sokbaoa,

“Aia Mama! Angade mamatangko dondape o gipako changbate chu 
ringchengani indake marang ong joki Mama angko kema ka-pabol* ine joljoi 
Salgra an tangan mamatangna chu kansoe gitdok chikgrikaha. 
Unonisa A chik manderang an-tangtangna dal batgipako dondape 
maming saloba songo noko chu chekoba ringot tapot dakja. Ba-skangkode 
pangnan salko jako nikehenggipa do ki wakki ga-dapehenggipa ine manda 
ra a. Jonggipa adatangko dondape ringja uandake groba jeja. Mamatang 
gnangode gritang gro jechengna man-ja, nima. 
Tom dakenggipaoni wanggipa ong-jaoba gumetang bilsi bribata ongoda 
uasa ringot tapota ong-jaoba rugala. Ua chu ringgijagipa ong-ode uni Jakrani 
ja si bimao rudapehenga. Apsandake mamatang gnangode indiba ua dui 
ringjaode uni ja-skilo rugalchenga. 
Apsan rama ming bidingko ja rike adatangna skang jonggipa 
kimehengja. Jonggipa jik kimehengode uko so-mal dona ina. Unoda adatangna 
gamcha gipbok patsa on-esa uko so mal gitdok chika. Unosa adatang mka 
nape cha-rim ringrima. Jonggipa adatangko dokmanoba adatangna so'inat 
gitdok chikna nanga.

10. SAMAN RAKGIPA 
Simsang chisam Samanda jolo chengo Toganna skangnian bilak 
wa-sagipako ua joini ma giparang bang en ba-toka ine janapani gnang. Ua 
Simsang chikoini mat chidualrangoni Rengram Do misal jeni chelbak miksku 
ong achim. Simsang wari jameskude*' uni auao gipsokjae janggil an-misalko 
rongreng rongreng nikpilachimna. Unide Boro Sanj^ng Doma Kejang boningsa 
matchadu anti cha dilgipako a-rong a sak mingsingangaha. Uni ja-mano 
atchirikgipa Saman Rakgipa Ruram sang gipaba ua Samanda sepang Simsang 
chisamo atchigipan ong achim. 
Ua chasongo Kal ak aro Samanda joiko Gra Manda Asil Danaini bilak 
wa sagipa Bikara Tobika Chelara Asanpa unia Sangma Ejim Sangma Ipak 
minggipa bobil dingna re bajringaiachim. Uamangni bobil dinge sko sote 
ra angani Kai ak songni manderang songgima nokgimana am pilachimna. 
Songni me asarangde bobil dingna amchakjae bon pilna am achim. Baditaba 
sak nomilrangsa ua bilakgipa bobil dingrakgipani jakoni joke buringo kate 
chaktokaha. A-bri damsachiko niatoba kalak kaltak me-ga bijakko nokking

pina gita nikataiachim. Una rikangode nikjachim. Una nigamtaiode a-paksachi 
kalak kaltak me gako pe gope ma-chakna noktop rika gita nikataiachim. 
Rikangode uchiba dongjachim. Indake me chikrangni ua bilak wa sagipa

su kape cha-engachim.

me chanchie uko kolna chakatna dakon on tisa wa se gam-maton dipblok 
rang san ua rong-teko sukape minokpile noksik gipinchi bile katangtaiachim.

nie rochina agane nokkreng sepangkale sakadoe dibplok ong-katako time 
nitaiaha.

A-bidingoni adita chel kale ong onangaha. Silang okkaini bolma bolgrim,

Uni ku rang on chaka tarime, 'wao wao wao' ku rang rakbatroroe gam baha.

jangbaenga ua chadengdike niwalwale knatimaha. Unoara sal naba 
dingdingchi, bolma kambe jikjek jikjek, bol bijakrang moa gita nikataha. Aditani 
ja-man ku rang jangbatbaha aro sepangrikrikbaha.

ga-dangdange re-baengachim. Ua bol ja-gok sorokrakgipachi nape chakaha,

34. KORE BIMA DAREKO MALA?

nomilrangni bimango parakgiparang jerangan buring bolgrim, a-brirang aro 
chirangrango donga. Jekai Nymph, Siren, Naiad, Dryad, Nareed, Oceanid, 
indakgiparang. Uarangoni bang-batan, basakoba, sagalo chogiparangko aiao 
inmanpile ka srokatgipa ku-rangchi ring-e gisikko jadipchangate an-tangtangko 
siaona sokatronga ine janapa.

38. BIPANG BiMANG PIL A? 
Mitamrangko sandirikkite niode ong na amgijani gita nika, indiba ia 
a gilsako mamingan ong na amgijani dongja. Ua ong na ama aro amgijaniko

bisi ong a.

ong-jaoba siaona sokata, ia aganritinganirangko A chik manderangni

bang e nikrongja. Indiba ua bibal nitoe balani gimin mitamrangni sa rao uko 
bibalna ge-ako nikna man ronga.

dingtanga. Ua dikgeko apsan dake nikoba bima unia bipa donga jeko
